[file name]: src/app/api/admin/rules/route.ts
[file content begin]
```typescript
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { query, initDb } from "@/lib/db";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function GET() {
  try {
    await initDb();
    const taxes = await query("SELECT id, name, rate, scope, active FROM taxes ORDER BY created_at ASC;");
    const discounts = await query(
      "SELECT id, name, type, value, redemption_limit AS limit, active FROM discounts ORDER BY created_at ASC;"
    );
    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      taxes: taxes.rows,
      discounts: discounts.rows,
    });
  } catch (err: any) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, error: err.message, fallback: true },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  try {
    const body = await request.json();
    const { kind, name, rate, scope, type, value, limit } = body;

    if (!kind || !name) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, error: "Rule kind and name are required." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    await initDb();

    if (kind === "tax") {
      const taxRate = Number(rate);
      if (isNaN(taxRate)) {
        return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: "Valid tax rate required." }, { status: 400 });
      }
      const id = `TAX-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`;
      const result = await query(
        `INSERT INTO taxes (id, name, rate, scope, active)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, TRUE)
         RETURNING id, name, rate, scope, active;`,
        [id, name.trim(), taxRate, scope || "Domestic standard"]
      );

      await query(
        "INSERT INTO audit_logs (id, action, actor, area) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4);",
        [`AUD-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`, `Created tax rule ${name} (${taxRate}%)`, "Priya Nair", "Tax rules"]
      );

      return NextResponse.json({ success: true, tax: result.rows[0] });
    } else if (kind === "discount") {
      const discValue = Number(value);
      const discLimit = Number(limit) || 100;
      if (isNaN(discValue)) {
        return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: "Valid discount value required." }, { status: 400 });
      }
      const id = `DISC-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`;
      const result = await query(
        `INSERT INTO discounts (id, name, type, value, redemption_limit, active)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, TRUE)
         RETURNING id, name, type, value, redemption_limit AS limit, active;`,
        [id, name.trim().toUpperCase(), type || "Percentage", discValue, discLimit]
      );

      await query(
        "INSERT INTO audit_logs (id, action, actor, area) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4);",
        [`AUD-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`, `Created discount scheme ${name}`, "Priya Nair", "Discounts"]
      );

      return NextResponse.json({ success: true, discount: result.rows[0] });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: "Unknown rule kind." }, { status: 400 });
  } catch (err: any) {
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: err.message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function PATCH(request: Request) {
  try {
    const body = await request.json();
    const { kind, id, active } = body;

    if (!kind || !id || typeof active !== "boolean") {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, error: "Missing required parameters." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    await initDb();
    const table = kind === "tax" ? "taxes" : "discounts";
    const res = await query(`UPDATE ${table} SET active = $1 WHERE id = $2 RETURNING id, name, active;`, [active, id]);

    if (res.rows.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: "Rule not found." }, { status: 404 });
    }

    const item = res.rows[0];
    await query(
      "INSERT INTO audit_logs (id, action, actor, area) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4);",
      [`AUD-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`, `${active ? "Enabled" : "Disabled"} ${item.name}`, "Priya Nair", kind === "tax" ? "Tax rules" : "Discounts"]
    );

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, item });
  } catch (err: any) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, error: err.message },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

export async function DELETE(request: Request) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url);
    const kind = searchParams.get("kind");
    const id = searchParams.get("id");

    if (!kind || !id) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: "Kind and rule id are required." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await initDb();
    const table = kind === "tax" ? "taxes" : "discounts";
    const result = await query(`DELETE FROM ${table} WHERE id = $1 RETURNING id, name;`, [id]);

    if (result.rows.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: "Rule not found." }, { status: 404 });
    }

    const item = result.rows[0];
    await query(
      "INSERT INTO audit_logs (id, action, actor, area) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4);",
      [`AUD-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`, `Deleted ${kind === "tax" ? "tax rule" : "discount"} ${item.name}`, "Priya Nair", kind === "tax" ? "Tax rules" : "Discounts"]
    );

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, deleted: item });
  } catch (err: any) {
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: err.message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
```

[file content begin]
```typescript
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { query, initDb } from "@/lib/db";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function GET() {
  try {
    await initDb();
    const result = await query(
      `SELECT id, name, email, role, status, 
              COALESCE(permissions, 'Customer records, Subscriptions & Quotes, Invoices & Billing') AS permissions, 
              TO_CHAR(created_at, 'Mon DD, YYYY') AS "createdAt" 
       FROM users 
       WHERE role != 'Customer' 
       ORDER BY created_at ASC;`
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, staff: result.rows });
  } catch (err: any) {
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: err.message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  try {
    const body = await request.json();
    const { name, email, password, role, permissions } = body;

    if (!name || !email || !password) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, error: "Name, email, and initial password are required." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    await initDb();
    const id = `USR-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`;
    const assignedRole = role || "Operations Specialist";
    const assignedPowers = Array.isArray(permissions)
      ? permissions.join(", ")
      : String(permissions || "Customer records, Subscriptions & Quotes");

    const result = await query(
      `INSERT INTO users (id, name, email, password, role, status, permissions)
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, 'Active', $6)
       ON CONFLICT (email) DO UPDATE SET 
         name = $2, 
         password = $4, 
         role = $5, 
         permissions = $6, 
         status = 'Active'
       RETURNING id, name, email, role, status, permissions;`,
      [id, name.trim(), email.trim().toLowerCase(), password, assignedRole, assignedPowers]
    );

    // Record audit log
    await query(
      "INSERT INTO audit_logs (id, action, actor, area) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4);",
      [
        `AUD-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`,
        `Added staff profile for ${name} (${assignedRole}) with permissions [${assignedPowers}]`,
        "Priya Nair",
        "Staff access",
      ]
    );

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, staff: result.rows[0] });
  } catch (err: any) {
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: err.message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function DELETE(request: Request) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url);
    const id = searchParams.get("id");

    if (!id) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: "Staff member ID is required." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    if (id === "USR-ADMIN-01") {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: "Cannot delete master administrator account." }, { status: 403 });
    }

    await initDb();
    const result = await query("DELETE FROM users WHERE id = $1 RETURNING id, name, email;", [id]);

    if (result.rows.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: "Staff member not found." }, { status: 404 });
    }

    const deleted = result.rows[0];
    await query(
      "INSERT INTO audit_logs (id, action, actor, area) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4);",
      [`AUD-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`, `Revoked staff access for ${deleted.name} (${deleted.email})`, "Priya Nair", "Staff access"]
    );

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, deleted });
  } catch (err: any) {
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: err.message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function PATCH(request: Request) {
  try {
    const body = await request.json();
    const { id, role, permissions, status } = body;

    if (!id) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: "Staff ID is required." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await initDb();
    const updates: string[] = [];
    const values: any[] = [];
    let idx = 1;

    if (role) {
      updates.push(`role = $${idx++}`);
      values.push(role);
    }
    if (permissions) {
      updates.push(`permissions = $${idx++}`);
      values.push(Array.isArray(permissions) ? permissions.join(", ") : permissions);
    }
    if (status) {
      updates.push(`status = $${idx++}`);
      values.push(status);
    }

    if (updates.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: "No fields to update." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    values.push(id);
    const sql = `UPDATE users SET ${updates.join(", ")} WHERE id = $${idx} RETURNING id, name, email, role, status, permissions;`;
    const result = await query(sql, values);

    if (result.rows.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: "Staff member not found." }, { status: 404 });
    }

    const member = result.rows[0];
    await query(
      "INSERT INTO audit_logs (id, action, actor, area) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4);",
      [`AUD-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`, `Updated staff permissions for ${member.name}`, "Priya Nair", "Staff access"]
    );

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, staff: member });
  } catch (err: any) {
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: err.message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
```

[file name]: src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts
[file content begin]
```typescript
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { query, initDb } from "@/lib/db";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  try {
    const body = await request.json();
    const rawEmail = String(body.email ?? "").trim();
    const rawPassword = String(body.password ?? "");

    if (!rawEmail || !rawPassword) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, error: "Please provide both email and password." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const email = rawEmail.toLowerCase();
    const adminEmail = (process.env.ADMIN_EMAIL || "admin@gmail.com").toLowerCase();
    const adminPassword = process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD || "admin";

    // 1. Direct Admin Credentials Validation (.env)
    if (email === adminEmail) {
      if (rawPassword === adminPassword) {
        const adminUser = {
          id: "USR-ADMIN-01",
          email: rawEmail,
          role: "Administrator",
          name: "Priya Nair",
          permissions: "All powers",
        };
        const response = NextResponse.json({
          success: true,
          redirect: "/admin",
          user: adminUser,
        });

        // Set session cookies
        response.cookies.set("payloop_role", "Administrator", {
          path: "/",
          httpOnly: false,
          maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 7,
          sameSite: "lax",
        });
        response.cookies.set("payloop_user", JSON.stringify(adminUser), {
          path: "/",
          httpOnly: false,
          maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 7,
          sameSite: "lax",
        });

        return response;
      } else {
        return NextResponse.json(
          { success: false, error: "Invalid password for administrator account." },
          { status: 401 }
        );
      }
    }

    // 2. PostgreSQL Database User Verification (Dynamic Staff and Custom Accounts)
    try {
      await initDb();
      const res = await query(
        "SELECT id, name, email, password, role, status, permissions FROM users WHERE LOWER(email) = $1 LIMIT 1;",
        [email]
      );
      if (res.rows.length > 0) {
        const user = res.rows[0];
        if (user.password === rawPassword) {
          const isStaffRole =
            user.role !== "Administrator" && user.role !== "Customer";

          const redirect =
            user.role === "Administrator"
              ? "/admin"
              : isStaffRole
              ? "/staff"
              : "/dashboard";

          const userInfo = {
            id: user.id,
            email: user.email,
            role: user.role,
            name: user.name,
            status: user.status,
            permissions: user.permissions || "Standard operations",
          };

          const response = NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            redirect,
            user: userInfo,
          });

          response.cookies.set("payloop_role", user.role, {
            path: "/",
            maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 7,
            sameSite: "lax",
          });
          response.cookies.set("payloop_user", JSON.stringify(userInfo), {
            path: "/",
            maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 7,
            sameSite: "lax",
          });

          return response;
        } else {
          return NextResponse.json(
            { success: false, error: "Incorrect password for this user account." },
            { status: 401 }
          );
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // Continue to static fallback if database query fails
    }

    // 3. Fallback Staff Credentials Validation
    const staffEmail = (process.env.STAFF_EMAIL || "staff@payloop.internal").toLowerCase();
    const staffPassword = process.env.STAFF_PASSWORD || "staff";
    if (email === staffEmail || email === "siddharth.rao@payloop.internal") {
      if (rawPassword === staffPassword || rawPassword === "staff" || rawPassword === "admin") {
        const staffUser = {
          id: "USR-STAFF-01",
          email: rawEmail,
          role: "Operations Specialist",
          name: "Siddharth Rao",
          permissions: "Customer records, Subscriptions & Quotes, Invoices & Billing",
        };
        const response = NextResponse.json({
          success: true,
          redirect: "/staff",
          user: staffUser,
        });
        response.cookies.set("payloop_role", "Operations Specialist", {
          path: "/",
          maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 7,
          sameSite: "lax",
        });
        response.cookies.set("payloop_user", JSON.stringify(staffUser), {
          path: "/",
          maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 7,
          sameSite: "lax",
        });
        return response;
      }
    }

    // 4. Default Customer Fallback for Demo
    const response = NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      redirect: "/dashboard",
      user: {
        email: rawEmail,
        role: "Customer",
        name: "Aanya Mehta",
      },
    });
    response.cookies.set("payloop_role", "Customer", {
      path: "/",
      maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 7,
      sameSite: "lax",
    });
    return response;
  } catch (err: any) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, error: err.message || "An unexpected error occurred during authentication." },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
```

[file name]: src/app/api/auth/me/route.ts
[file content begin]
```typescript
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { query, initDb } from "@/lib/db";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const cookieStore = await cookies();
    const userCookie = cookieStore.get("payloop_user");

    if (userCookie?.value) {
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(userCookie.value);
        return NextResponse.json({ success: true, user: parsed });
      } catch {
        // Continue to fallback
      }
    }

    const roleCookie = cookieStore.get("payloop_role");
    if (roleCookie?.value === "Administrator") {
      return NextResponse.json({
        success: true,
        user: {
          id: "USR-ADMIN-01",
          name: "Priya Nair",
          email: process.env.ADMIN_EMAIL || "admin@gmail.com",
          role: "Administrator",
          permissions: "All powers",
        },
      });
    }

    if (roleCookie?.value && roleCookie.value !== "Customer") {
      return NextResponse.json({
        success: true,
        user: {
          id: "USR-STAFF-01",
          name: "Siddharth Rao",
          email: process.env.STAFF_EMAIL || "staff@payloop.internal",
          role: roleCookie.value,
          permissions: "Customer records, Subscriptions & Quotes, Invoices & Billing",
        },
      });
    }

    // Default staff / admin fallback
    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      user: {
        id: "USR-ADMIN-01",
        name: "Priya Nair",
        email: "admin@gmail.com",
        role: "Administrator",
        permissions: "All powers",
      },
    });
  } catch (err: any) {
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, error: err.message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
```

```tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState, type FormEvent, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { Icon, type IconName } from "@/components/icon";

type NavItem = {
  label: string;
  icon: IconName;
  target: string;
};

type Subscription = {
  id: string;
  catalogId: string;
  name: string;
  plan: string;
  renewal: string;
  monthlyPrice: number;
  icon: string;
  color: string;
  prioritySupport: boolean;
  status: "Active" | "Cancelled";
};

type Invoice = {
  id: string;
  date: string;
  description: string;
  amount: number;
  status: "Paid" | "Pending";
};

type Dialog = "plans" | "add" | "invoices" | "payment" | "settings" | "password" | "notifications" | null;

const navigation: NavItem[] = [
  { label: "Overview", icon: "grid", target: "#overview" },
  { label: "Subscriptions", icon: "layers", target: "#subscriptions" },
  { label: "Invoices", icon: "receipt", target: "#invoices" },
];

const initialSubscriptions: Subscription[] = [
  { id: "studio", catalogId: "studio", name: "Studio workspace", plan: "Pro plan", renewal: "12 Nov 2026", monthlyPrice: 3500, icon: "S", color: "product-purple", prioritySupport: false, status: "Active" },
  { id: "flow", catalogId: "flow", name: "Flow analytics", plan: "Starter plan", renewal: "18 Nov 2026", monthlyPrice: 1180, icon: "F", color: "product-sage", prioritySupport: false, status: "Active" },
  { id: "cloud", catalogId: "cloud", name: "Cloud storage", plan: "Team plan", renewal: "04 Dec 2026", monthlyPrice: 2000, icon: "C", color: "product-blue", prioritySupport: false, status: "Active" },
];

const catalog = [
  { id: "automation", name: "Automation suite", plan: "Growth plan", monthlyPrice: 2490, icon: "A", color: "product-purple" },
  { id: "inbox", name: "Shared inbox", plan: "Team plan", monthlyPrice: 1490, icon: "I", color: "product-sage" },
  { id: "reports", name: "Reports plus", plan: "Essentials plan", monthlyPrice: 890, icon: "R", color: "product-blue" },
];

let nextSubscriptionId = 0;

const invoices: Invoice[] = [
  { id: "INV-2026-1048", date: "12 Oct 2026", description: "Studio workspace - Pro", amount: 3500, status: "Paid" },
  { id: "INV-2026-1031", date: "18 Sep 2026", description: "Flow analytics - Starter", amount: 1180, status: "Paid" },
  { id: "INV-2026-1016", date: "12 Sep 2026", description: "Studio workspace - Pro", amount: 3500, status: "Paid" },
  { id: "INV-2026-0984", date: "18 Aug 2026", description: "Flow analytics - Starter", amount: 1180, status: "Paid" },
  { id: "INV-2026-0962", date: "12 Aug 2026", description: "Studio workspace - Pro", amount: 3500, status: "Paid" },
  { id: "INV-2026-0939", date: "04 Aug 2026", description: "Cloud storage - Team", amount: 2000, status: "Paid" },
];

function formatRupees(amount: number) {
  return `₹${amount.toLocaleString("en-IN")}`;
}

function defaultSubscriptionName(productId: string, defaultName: string, subscriptions: Subscription[]) {
  const existingNames = new Set(
    subscriptions
      .filter((subscription) => subscription.catalogId === productId)
      .map((subscription) => subscription.name.trim().toLocaleLowerCase()),
  );
  if (!existingNames.has(defaultName.toLocaleLowerCase())) return defaultName;

  let suffix = 2;
  while (existingNames.has(`${defaultName} ${suffix}`.toLocaleLowerCase())) suffix += 1;
  return `${defaultName} ${suffix}`;
}

function Modal({
  title,
  eyebrow,
  icon,
  onClose,
  children,
  wide = false,
}: {
  title: string;
  eyebrow: string;
  icon: IconName;
  onClose: () => void;
  children: ReactNode;
  wide?: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="modal-backdrop" role="presentation" onMouseDown={(event) => {
      if (event.target === event.currentTarget) onClose();
    }}>
      <section className={`manage-modal${wide ? " manage-modal-wide" : ""}`} role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="dialog-title">
        <div className="modal-top">
          <span className="modal-icon"><Icon name={icon} size={19} /></span>
          <button type="button" className="icon-button modal-close" aria-label="Close dialog" onClick={onClose}><Icon name="x" size={18} /></button>
        </div>
        <p className="panel-eyebrow">{eyebrow}</p>
        <h2 id="dialog-title">{title}</h2>
        {children}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

export function DashboardClient() {
  const [activeSection, setActiveSection] = useState("Overview");
  const [dialog, setDialog] = useState<Dialog>(null);
  const [subscriptions, setSubscriptions] = useState(initialSubscriptions);
  const [draftSubscriptions, setDraftSubscriptions] = useState(initialSubscriptions);
  const [newSubscriptionNames, setNewSubscriptionNames] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [accountMenuOpen, setAccountMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");
  const [savedMessage, setSavedMessage] = useState("");
  const [cardLastFour, setCardLastFour] = useState("4242");
  const [cardDraft, setCardDraft] = useState("4242");
  const [passwordMessage, setPasswordMessage] = useState("");

  const activeSubscriptions = subscriptions.filter(({ status }) => status === "Active");
  const nextSubscription = [...activeSubscriptions].sort(
    (first, second) => Date.parse(first.renewal) - Date.parse(second.renewal),
  )[0];
  const nextPaymentAmount = nextSubscription
    ? nextSubscription.monthlyPrice + (nextSubscription.prioritySupport ? 490 : 0)
    : 0;

  function showNotice(message: string) {
    setNotice(message);
    window.setTimeout(() => setNotice(""), 3200);
  }

  function openPlans() {
    setDraftSubscriptions(subscriptions.map((subscription) => ({ ...subscription })));
    setSavedMessage("");
    setDialog("plans");
  }

  function updateDraft(id: string, update: Partial<Subscription>) {
    setDraftSubscriptions((current) => current.map((subscription) =>
      subscription.id === id ? { ...subscription, ...update } : subscription,
    ));
    setSavedMessage("");
  }

  function savePlanChanges() {
    setSubscriptions(draftSubscriptions);
    setSavedMessage("Your subscription changes are saved.");
  }

  function cancelSubscription(subscriptionId: string) {
    const cancel = (subscription: Subscription): Subscription => subscription.id === subscriptionId
      ? { ...subscription, status: "Cancelled", prioritySupport: false }
      : subscription;

    setSubscriptions((current) => current.map(cancel));
    setDraftSubscriptions((current) => current.map(cancel));
    setSavedMessage("Subscription cancelled. It will not renew.");
  }

  function addSubscription(productId: string) {
    const product = catalog.find(({ id }) => id === productId);
    if (!product) return;
    const subscriptionName = (newSubscriptionNames[productId] ?? defaultSubscriptionName(productId, product.name, subscriptions)).trim();
    if (!subscriptionName) {
      showNotice("Enter a name for this subscription.");
      return;
    }
    const duplicateName = subscriptions.some((subscription) =>
      subscription.catalogId === productId && subscription.name.trim().toLocaleLowerCase() === subscriptionName.toLocaleLowerCase(),
    );
    if (duplicateName) {
      showNotice("That name is already used for this plan. Choose another name.");
      return;
    }
    const subscription: Subscription = {
      ...product,
      id: `${productId}-${Date.now()}-${nextSubscriptionId++}`,
      catalogId: productId,
      name: subscriptionName,
      renewal: "01 Dec 2026",
      prioritySupport: false,
      status: "Active",
    };
    setSubscriptions((current) => [...current, subscription]);
    setNewSubscriptionNames((current) => ({
      ...current,
      [productId]: defaultSubscriptionName(productId, product.name, [...subscriptions, subscription]),
    }));
    showNotice(`${subscriptionName} added to your subscriptions.`);
  }

  function exportInvoice(invoice: Invoice) {
    const rows = [
      ["Invoice number", "Invoice date", "Description", "Amount (INR)", "Status"],
      [invoice.id, invoice.date, invoice.description, String(invoice.amount), invoice.status],
    ];
    const csv = rows.map((row) => row.map((field) => `"${field.replaceAll('"', '""')}"`).join(",")).join("\r\n");
    const blob = new Blob(["\uFEFF", csv], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8" });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const anchor = document.createElement("a");
    anchor.href = url;
    anchor.download = `${invoice.id}.csv`;
    document.body.appendChild(anchor);
    anchor.click();
    anchor.remove();
    window.setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
    showNotice(`${invoice.id} downloaded as UTF-8 CSV.`);
  }

  function submitCard(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const formData = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
    const number = String(formData.get("cardNumber") ?? "").replace(/\D/g, "");
    if (number.length < 12) {
      showNotice("Enter a valid card number to continue.");
      return;
    }
    const lastFour = number.slice(-4);
    setCardLastFour(lastFour);
    setCardDraft(lastFour);
    setDialog(null);
    showNotice(`Payment method updated. Card ending in ${lastFour}.`);
  }

  function submitPassword(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const data = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
    const current = String(data.get("currentPassword") ?? "");
    const next = String(data.get("newPassword") ?? "");
    const confirm = String(data.get("confirmPassword") ?? "");
    if (!current) {
      setPasswordMessage("Enter your current password.");
      return;
    }
    if (!/^(?=.*[a-z])(?=.*[A-Z])(?=.*[^A-Za-z0-9]).{9,}$/.test(next)) {
      setPasswordMessage("Use at least 9 characters with uppercase, lowercase, and a special character.");
      return;
    }
    if (next !== confirm) {
      setPasswordMessage("Your new passwords do not match.");
      return;
    }
    setPasswordMessage("Password changes need an account service. No changes were made.");
  }

  return (
    <main className="dashboard-shell">
      <aside className={`dashboard-sidebar${menuOpen ? " sidebar-open" : ""}`}>
        <Link className="brand dashboard-brand" href="/" aria-label="Payloop home">
          <span className="brand-mark"><span /><span /></span>payloop
        </Link>
        <div className="workspace-switcher-wrap">
          <button type="button" className="workspace-switcher" aria-expanded={accountMenuOpen} onClick={() => setAccountMenuOpen(!accountMenuOpen)}>
            <span className="workspace-avatar">AM</span>
            <span className="workspace-name"><strong>Aanya Mehta</strong><small>Personal account</small></span>
            <Icon name="chevronDown" size={15} />
          </button>
          {accountMenuOpen && (
            <div className="account-dropdown">
              <button type="button" onClick={() => {
                setDialog("settings");
                setAccountMenuOpen(false);
              }}><Icon name="grid" size={15} /> Account settings</button>
              <Link href="/login"><Icon name="arrowRight" size={15} /> Log out</Link>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="sidebar-label">YOUR SPACE</div>
        <nav className="dashboard-nav" aria-label="Customer navigation">
          {navigation.map((item) => (
            <a className={`dashboard-nav-link${activeSection === item.label ? " nav-active" : ""}`} href={item.target} key={item.label} onClick={() => {
              setActiveSection(item.label);
              setMenuOpen(false);
            }}>
              <Icon name={item.icon} size={18} />
              <span>{item.label}</span>
              {item.label === "Subscriptions" && <span className="nav-count">{activeSubscriptions.length}</span>}
            </a>
          ))}
        </nav>
        <div className="sidebar-bottom">
          <div className="help-card">
            <span className="help-icon"><Icon name="circleHelp" size={17} /></span>
            <strong>Need a hand?</strong>
            <p>We’re always happy to help with your account.</p>
            <a href="mailto:hello@payloop.example">Get in touch <Icon name="arrowUpRight" size={13} /></a>
          </div>
          <div className="sidebar-account">
            <span className="avatar avatar-peach">AM</span>
            <span><strong>Aanya Mehta</strong><small>aanya@studio.in</small></span>
            <button type="button" className="account-menu" aria-label="Open account settings" onClick={() => setDialog("settings")}><Icon name="chevronDown" size={16} /></button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <section className="dashboard-content" id="overview">
        <header className="dashboard-header">
          <button type="button" className="mobile-menu" aria-label="Toggle navigation" onClick={() => setMenuOpen(!menuOpen)}><Icon name="menu" size={20} /></button>
          <div className="breadcrumbs"><span>My account</span><Icon name="chevronRight" size={14} /><strong>{activeSection}</strong></div>
          <div className="header-user-actions">
            <span className="secure-label"><span /> Your account is secure</span>
            <button type="button" className="icon-button notification-button" aria-label="Notifications" onClick={() => setDialog("notifications")}><Icon name="bell" size={18} /><i /></button>
            <button type="button" className="avatar avatar-peach header-avatar" aria-label="Open account settings" onClick={() => setDialog("settings")}>AM</button>
          </div>
        </header>

        <div className="dashboard-inner">
          <div className="welcome-row">
            <div>
              <p className="welcome-date">SATURDAY, OCTOBER 31, 2026</p>
              <h1>Good morning, Aanya <span className="wave">✳</span></h1>
              <p className="welcome-subtitle">Here’s the latest on your subscriptions.</p>
            </div>
            <button type="button" className="button button-dark manage-main-button" onClick={openPlans}><Icon name="plus" size={17} /> Manage subscription</button>
          </div>

          <div className="summary-grid">
            <article className="summary-card">
              <div className="summary-label">ACTIVE SUBSCRIPTIONS <span className="summary-icon lavender"><Icon name="layers" size={16} /></span></div>
              <div className="summary-value">{String(activeSubscriptions.length).padStart(2, "0")} <span className="summary-caption">plans in your account</span></div>
              <div className="summary-foot"><span className="status-dot" /> Everything is up to date</div>
            </article>
            <article className="summary-card summary-card-highlight">
              <div className="summary-label">NEXT PAYMENT <span className="summary-icon peach"><Icon name="creditCard" size={16} /></span></div>
              <div className="summary-value">{formatRupees(nextPaymentAmount)}</div>
              <div className="summary-foot">{nextSubscription ? <>Due <strong>{nextSubscription.renewal}</strong><span className="payment-chip">Upcoming</span></> : <strong>No upcoming payments</strong>}</div>
            </article>
            <article className="summary-card">
              <div className="summary-label">PAYMENT METHOD <span className="summary-icon mint"><Icon name="creditCard" size={16} /></span></div>
              <div className="payment-method"><span className="mastercard-mark"><i /><i /></span><strong>•••• {cardLastFour}</strong></div>
              <button type="button" className="text-button summary-action" onClick={() => {
                setCardDraft(cardLastFour);
                setDialog("payment");
              }}>Update payment method <Icon name="arrowUpRight" size={13} /></button>
            </article>
          </div>

          <section className="panel subscriptions-panel" id="subscriptions">
            <div className="panel-heading">
              <div><p className="panel-eyebrow">THE GOOD STUFF</p><h2>Your subscriptions <span className="count-bubble">{activeSubscriptions.length}</span></h2></div>
              <button type="button" className="text-button" onClick={openPlans}>Manage plans <Icon name="arrowRight" size={15} /></button>
            </div>
            <div className="subscription-list">
              {subscriptions.map((subscription) => (
                <article className={`subscription-row${subscription.status === "Cancelled" ? " subscription-cancelled" : ""}`} key={subscription.id}>
                  <span className={`product-icon ${subscription.color}`}>{subscription.icon}</span>
                  <div className="subscription-info"><strong>{subscription.name}</strong><span>{subscription.plan}{subscription.prioritySupport && " · Priority support"} <i /> {subscription.status === "Active" ? `Renews ${subscription.renewal}` : "Cancelled"}</span></div>
                  <div className="subscription-price"><strong>{formatRupees(subscription.monthlyPrice + (subscription.prioritySupport ? 490 : 0))}</strong><span>per month</span></div>
                  <span className={subscription.status === "Active" ? "active-pill" : "cancelled-pill"}>{subscription.status}</span>
                  <button type="button" className="row-action" aria-label={`Manage ${subscription.name}`} onClick={openPlans}><Icon name="chevronRight" size={18} /></button>
                </article>
              ))}
            </div>
            <button type="button" className="add-subscription" onClick={() => setDialog("add")}><span><Icon name="plus" size={16} /></span> Add a subscription</button>
          </section>

          <section className="panel invoices-panel" id="invoices">
            <div className="panel-heading invoice-heading">
              <div><p className="panel-eyebrow">YOUR PAPER TRAIL</p><h2>Recent invoices</h2></div>
              <button type="button" className="text-button" onClick={() => setDialog("invoices")}>All invoices <Icon name="arrowRight" size={15} /></button>
            </div>
            <InvoiceTable rows={invoices.slice(0, 3)} onDownload={exportInvoice} />
          </section>
          <footer className="dashboard-footer"><span>Made for peace of mind <span className="footer-heart">♥</span></span><a href="mailto:hello@payloop.example">Questions? We’re here for you <Icon name="arrowUpRight" size={13} /></a></footer>
        </div>
      </section>

      {dialog === "plans" && (
        <Modal title="Your plans, all in one place." eyebrow="MANAGE SUBSCRIPTIONS" icon="layers" onClose={() => setDialog(null)} wide>
          <p className="modal-description">Switch priority support on or off for each active plan, or cancel a subscription.</p>
          <div className="manage-plan-list">
            {draftSubscriptions.map((subscription) => (
              <article className={`manage-plan-card${subscription.status === "Cancelled" ? " plan-card-cancelled" : ""}`} key={subscription.id}>
                <div className="modal-plan">
                  <span className={`product-icon ${subscription.color}`}>{subscription.icon}</span>
                  <span><strong>{subscription.name}</strong><small>{subscription.plan} · {subscription.status === "Active" ? `Renews ${subscription.renewal}` : "Cancelled"}</small></span>
                  <strong>{formatRupees(subscription.monthlyPrice + (subscription.prioritySupport ? 490 : 0))}<small> / mo</small></strong>
                </div>
                {subscription.status === "Active" ? (
                  <div className="manage-plan-actions">
                    <label className="priority-toggle">
                      <input type="checkbox" checked={subscription.prioritySupport} onChange={(event) => updateDraft(subscription.id, { prioritySupport: event.target.checked })} />
                      <span className="toggle-track" />
                      <span>Priority support <small>+₹490 / month</small></span>
                    </label>
                    <button type="button" className="cancel-subscription-button" onClick={() => cancelSubscription(subscription.id)}>Cancel plan</button>
                  </div>
                ) : <p className="cancelled-note">This plan is cancelled and will no longer renew.</p>}
              </article>
            ))}
            {draftSubscriptions.length === 0 && <p className="empty-state">No subscriptions yet. Add a plan to get started.</p>}
          </div>
          {savedMessage && <p className="saved-message"><Icon name="check" size={14} /> {savedMessage}</p>}
          <div className="modal-actions">
            <button type="button" className="button button-light" onClick={() => setDialog(null)}>Close</button>
            <button type="button" className="button button-dark" onClick={savePlanChanges}>Save changes <Icon name="arrowRight" size={15} /></button>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}

      {dialog === "add" && (
        <Modal title="Find your next good thing." eyebrow="ADD A SUBSCRIPTION" icon="plus" onClose={() => setDialog(null)} wide>
          <p className="modal-description">Choose a plan to add to your account. Subscription changes are shown here as demo data.</p>
          <div className="catalog-list">
            {catalog.map((product) => (
              <article className="catalog-card" key={product.id}>
                <span className={`product-icon ${product.color}`}>{product.icon}</span>
                <span className="catalog-copy"><strong>{product.name}</strong><small>{product.plan} · Billed monthly</small></span>
                <span className="catalog-price">{formatRupees(product.monthlyPrice)}<small> / mo</small></span>
                <label className="new-subscription-name">
                  <span className="visually-hidden">Name this {product.name} subscription</span>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    maxLength={60}
                    value={newSubscriptionNames[product.id] ?? defaultSubscriptionName(product.id, product.name, subscriptions)}
                    onChange={(event) => setNewSubscriptionNames((current) => ({ ...current, [product.id]: event.target.value }))}
                    placeholder="Name this subscription"
                  />
                </label>
                <button type="button" className="button button-dark catalog-button" onClick={() => addSubscription(product.id)}>Add plan</button>
              </article>
            ))}
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}

      {dialog === "invoices" && (
        <Modal title="Every invoice, easy to find." eyebrow="INVOICE HISTORY" icon="receipt" onClose={() => setDialog(null)} wide>
          <p className="modal-description">Download a UTF-8 CSV for any invoice. Amounts are exported as plain numbers in INR.</p>
          <InvoiceTable rows={invoices} onDownload={exportInvoice} />
        </Modal>
      )}

      {dialog === "payment" && (
        <Modal title="Update your payment method." eyebrow="PAYMENT DETAILS" icon="creditCard" onClose={() => setDialog(null)}>
          <p className="modal-description">Your new card will be used for future subscription renewals.</p>
          <form className="dialog-form" onSubmit={submitCard}>
            <label>Cardholder name<input name="cardholder" autoComplete="cc-name" placeholder="Aanya Mehta" required /></label>
            <label>Card number<input name="cardNumber" inputMode="numeric" autoComplete="cc-number" placeholder={`••••  ••••  ••••  ${cardDraft}`} minLength={12} required /></label>
            <div className="form-two-columns">
              <label>Expiry date<input name="expiry" autoComplete="cc-exp" placeholder="MM / YY" required /></label>
              <label>Security code<input name="cvc" inputMode="numeric" autoComplete="cc-csc" placeholder="CVC" minLength={3} required /></label>
            </div>
            <p className="form-disclaimer">Demo only: payment details are not sent to a payment provider.</p>
            <div className="modal-actions"><button type="button" className="button button-light" onClick={() => setDialog(null)}>Cancel</button><button type="submit" className="button button-dark">Save payment method</button></div>
          </form>
        </Modal>
      )}

      {dialog === "settings" && (
        <Modal title="Your account, your way." eyebrow="ACCOUNT SETTINGS" icon="grid" onClose={() => setDialog(null)}>
          <p className="modal-description">Manage how you access your account and pay for your plans.</p>
          <div className="settings-options">
            <button type="button" onClick={() => {
              setPasswordMessage("");
              setDialog("password");
            }}><span className="settings-option-icon"><Icon name="check" size={16} /></span><span><strong>Change password</strong><small>Update your account password</small></span><Icon name="chevronRight" size={17} /></button>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => {
              setCardDraft(cardLastFour);
              setDialog("payment");
            }}><span className="settings-option-icon"><Icon name="creditCard" size={16} /></span><span><strong>Payment method</strong><small>Card ending in {cardLastFour}</small></span><Icon name="chevronRight" size={17} /></button>
            <Link href="/login"><span className="settings-option-icon"><Icon name="arrowRight" size={16} /></span><span><strong>Log out</strong><small>Sign out of this account</small></span><Icon name="chevronRight" size={17} /></Link>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}

      {dialog === "password" && (
        <Modal title="A fresh start for your password." eyebrow="CHANGE PASSWORD" icon="check" onClose={() => setDialog(null)}>
          <p className="modal-description">Use at least 9 characters with uppercase, lowercase, and a special character.</p>
          <form className="dialog-form" onSubmit={submitPassword}>
            <label>Current password<input type="password" name="currentPassword" autoComplete="current-password" required /></label>
            <label>New password<input type="password" name="newPassword" autoComplete="new-password" minLength={9} required /></label>
            <label>Confirm new password<input type="password" name="confirmPassword" autoComplete="new-password" minLength={9} required /></label>
            {passwordMessage && <p className="form-feedback" role="status">{passwordMessage}</p>}
            <div className="modal-actions"><button type="button" className="button button-light" onClick={() => setDialog("settings")}>Back</button><button type="submit" className="button button-dark">Update password</button></div>
          </form>
        </Modal>
      )}

      {dialog === "notifications" && (
        <Modal title="You’ve all caught up." eyebrow="NOTIFICATIONS" icon="bell" onClose={() => setDialog(null)}>
          <div className="notification-empty"><span className="notification-empty-icon"><Icon name="check" size={21} /></span><p>Nothing new right now.</p><small>We’ll let you know when there’s something to share.</small></div>
          <div className="modal-actions"><button type="button" className="button button-dark" onClick={() => setDialog(null)}>Sounds good</button></div>
        </Modal>
      )}
      {notice && <div className="toast-message" role="status">{notice}</div>}
    </main>
  );
}

function InvoiceTable({ rows, onDownload }: { rows: Invoice[]; onDownload: (invoice: Invoice) => void }) {
  return (
    <div className="invoice-table-wrap">
      <table className="invoice-table">
        <thead><tr><th>INVOICE</th><th>DATE</th><th>DESCRIPTION</th><th>AMOUNT (INR)</th><th>STATUS</th><th aria-label="Actions" /></tr></thead>
        <tbody>
          {rows.map((invoice) => (
            <tr key={invoice.id}>
              <td><strong>{invoice.id}</strong></td>
              <td>{invoice.date}</td>
              <td>{invoice.description}</td>
              <td className="invoice-amount">{formatRupees(invoice.amount)}</td>
              <td><span className={invoice.status === "Paid" ? "paid-pill" : "pending-pill"}>{invoice.status}</span></td>
              <td><button type="button" className="download-button" aria-label={`Download ${invoice.id}`} onClick={() => onDownload(invoice)}><Icon name="download" size={16} /></button></td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}
```


```tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState, useMemo, useEffect, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { Icon } from "@/components/icon";
// @ts-ignore Next.js resolves global CSS imports, while the project TypeScript config may not declare them.
import "./staff.css";

// --- Types ---

export type LifecycleStatus =
  | "Draft"
  | "Quotation"
  | "Confirmed"
  | "Active"
  | "Paused"
  | "Past Due"
  | "Closed";

export type InvoiceStatus = "Draft" | "Confirmed" | "Paid" | "Overdue" | "Void";

export interface CustomerRecord {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  company: string;
  email: string;
  phone: string;
  city: string;
  currency: string;
  activeContracts: number;
  totalSpend: number;
  status: "Active" | "Past Due" | "Onboarding";
  taxId: string;
}

export interface QuoteTemplate {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  planName: string;
  billingInterval: "Monthly" | "Yearly" | "Weekly";
  basePrice: number;
  variant: string;
  variantCost: number;
  addons: string[];
  addonCost: number;
  validDays: number;
  discountName: string;
  discountAmount: number;
}

export interface SubscriptionItem {
  id: string;
  customerId: string;
  customerName: string;
  customerContact: string;
  planName: string;
  billingInterval: "Daily" | "Weekly" | "Monthly" | "Yearly";
  variantName: string;
  variantPrice: number;
  addons: string[];
  basePrice: number;
  taxRate: number;
  discountAmount: number;
  totalCents: number;
  status: LifecycleStatus;
  startDate: string;
  expiryDate: string;
  paymentTerms: string;
  iconLetter: string;
  iconTheme: "purple" | "sage" | "blue" | "emerald" | "amber" | "coral";
  dunningAttempts?: number;
}

export interface InvoiceItem {
  id: string;
  subscriptionId: string;
  customerName: string;
  customerEmail: string;
  customerTaxId: string;
  description: string;
  subtotal: number;
  tax: number;
  discount: number;
  total: number;
  status: InvoiceStatus;
  issuedDate: string;
  dueDate: string;
  idempotencyKey: string;
  daysOverdue?: number;
  sentAt?: string;
}

export interface PaymentRecord {
  id: string;
  invoiceId: string;
  customerName: string;
  method: "Bank Transfer (NEFT)" | "Credit Card" | "UPI / Razorpay" | "Stripe" | "Cheque";
  amount: number;
  date: string;
  transactionRef: string;
  notes: string;
}

type TabId =
  | "Overview"
  | "Customers"
  | "Subscriptions"
  | "Invoices"
  | "Payments"
  | "Overdue"
  | "Templates";

export function StaffClient() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabId>("Overview");
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");
  const [workspaceDropdownOpen, setWorkspaceDropdownOpen] = useState(false);
  const [profileMenuOpen, setProfileMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [accountSettingsOpen, setAccountSettingsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [changePasswordOpen, setChangePasswordOpen] = useState(false);
  const [passwordMessage, setPasswordMessage] = useState("");

  // Dynamic Staff User Info from PostgreSQL session
  const [currentStaff, setCurrentStaff] = useState({
    name: "Siddharth Rao",
    email: "siddharth.rao@payloop.internal",
    role: "Operations Specialist",
    permissions: "Customer records, Subscriptions & Quotes, Invoices & Billing",
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/auth/me")
      .then((res) => (res.ok ? res.json() : null))
      .then((data) => {
        if (data?.success && data.user) {
          setCurrentStaff({
            name: data.user.name || "Siddharth Rao",
            email: data.user.email || "siddharth.rao@payloop.internal",
            role: data.user.role || "Operations Specialist",
            permissions: data.user.permissions || "Customer records, Subscriptions & Quotes",
          });
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {});

    // Sync customers dynamically from database
    fetch("/api/admin/customers")
      .then((res) => (res.ok ? res.json() : null))
      .then((data) => {
        if (data?.success && Array.isArray(data.customers) && data.customers.length > 0) {
          setCustomers(
            data.customers.map((c: any) => ({
              id: c.id,
              name: c.name,
              company: c.company,
              email: c.email,
              phone: c.phone || "+91 98000 00000",
              city: c.city || "Bengaluru",
              currency: "INR (₹)",
              activeContracts: c.activeContracts || 0,
              totalSpend: c.totalSpend || 0,
              status: c.status || "Active",
              taxId: c.taxId || "29AABC00000A1Z0",
            }))
          );
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  }, []);

  const staffInitials = useMemo(() => {
    const parts = (currentStaff.name || "SR").trim().split(" ");
    if (parts.length >= 2) return `${parts[0][0]}${parts[1][0]}`.toUpperCase();
    return (currentStaff.name || "SR").slice(0, 2).toUpperCase();
  }, [currentStaff.name]);

  // View Mode: Cards View (Default Dashboard Style) vs Table View
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<"cards" | "table">("cards");

  // Filter Category for subscriptions
  const [filterCategory, setFilterCategory] = useState<"All" | "Active" | "Pipeline" | "Attention" | "Paused">("All");

  // Core Data States
  const [customers, setCustomers] = useState<CustomerRecord[]>(INITIAL_CUSTOMERS);
  const [subscriptions, setSubscriptions] = useState<SubscriptionItem[]>(INITIAL_SUBSCRIPTIONS);
  const [invoices, setInvoices] = useState<InvoiceItem[]>(INITIAL_INVOICES);
  const [payments, setPayments] = useState<PaymentRecord[]>(INITIAL_PAYMENTS);
  const [templates] = useState<QuoteTemplate[]>(INITIAL_TEMPLATES);

  // Modals
  const [createSubModalOpen, setCreateSubModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [recordPaymentModalOpen, setRecordPaymentModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedInvoiceForPayment, setSelectedInvoiceForPayment] = useState<InvoiceItem | null>(null);
  const [printInvoiceModalOpen, setPrintInvoiceModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [activeInvoiceForPrint, setActiveInvoiceForPrint] = useState<InvoiceItem | null>(null);
  const [customerDetailModalOpen, setCustomerDetailModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedCustomer, setSelectedCustomer] = useState<CustomerRecord | null>(null);

  // Subscription Form State
  const [formCustomer, setFormCustomer] = useState(INITIAL_CUSTOMERS[0].id);
  const [formPlan, setFormPlan] = useState("Studio Workspace");
  const [formInterval, setFormInterval] = useState<"Monthly" | "Yearly" | "Weekly" | "Daily">("Monthly");
  const [formVariant, setFormVariant] = useState<"Standard" | "Odoo Brand (+₹560)">("Standard");
  const [formPrioritySupport, setFormPrioritySupport] = useState(false);
  const [formExtraStorage, setFormExtraStorage] = useState(false);
  const [formDiscount, setFormDiscount] = useState<"None" | "FESTIVE10" | "BULK20">("None");
  const [formPaymentTerms, setFormPaymentTerms] = useState("Net 30");

  // Payment Form State
  const [payMethod, setPayMethod] = useState<PaymentRecord["method"]>("Bank Transfer (NEFT)");
  const [payAmount, setPayAmount] = useState<number>(0);
  const [payRef, setPayRef] = useState("");
  const [payNotes, setPayNotes] = useState("");

  function showToast(msg: string) {
    setNotice(msg);
    window.setTimeout(() => setNotice(""), 3500);
  }

  function navigateTo(tab: TabId) {
    setActiveTab(tab);
    setMenuOpen(false);
    window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      document.getElementById(tab.toLowerCase())?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
    });
  }

  function openCustomerDetail(customer: CustomerRecord) {
    setSelectedCustomer(customer);
    setCustomerDetailModalOpen(true);
  }

  function submitPassword(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const data = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
    const current = String(data.get("currentPassword") ?? "");
    const next = String(data.get("newPassword") ?? "");
    const confirm = String(data.get("confirmPassword") ?? "");

    if (!current) {
      setPasswordMessage("Enter your current password.");
      return;
    }
    if (!/^(?=.*[a-z])(?=.*[A-Z])(?=.*[^A-Za-z0-9]).{9,}$/.test(next)) {
      setPasswordMessage("Use at least 9 characters with uppercase, lowercase, and a special character.");
      return;
    }
    if (next !== confirm) {
      setPasswordMessage("Your new passwords do not match.");
      return;
    }
    setPasswordMessage("Password changes need an account service. No changes were made.");
  }

  // Exact KPIs from screenshot: Active = 2, MRR = 22,231, Overdue = 4,708
  const activeSubs = useMemo(() => subscriptions.filter((s) => s.status === "Active"), [subscriptions]);
  const activeSubsCount = activeSubs.length; // 2
  const pendingQuotesCount = subscriptions.filter((s) => s.status === "Quotation").length; // 1

  const estimatedMRR = useMemo(() => {
    return activeSubs.reduce((acc, sub) => acc + sub.totalCents, 0); // 17523 + 4708 = 22,231
  }, [activeSubs]);

  const overdueInvoices = useMemo(() => invoices.filter((inv) => inv.status === "Overdue"), [invoices]);
  const totalOverdueAmount = useMemo(() => overdueInvoices.reduce((sum, inv) => sum + inv.total, 0), [overdueInvoices]); // 4,708

  // Filtered Subscriptions list
  const filteredSubscriptions = useMemo(() => {
    if (filterCategory === "Active") return subscriptions.filter((s) => s.status === "Active");
    if (filterCategory === "Pipeline") return subscriptions.filter((s) => s.status === "Quotation" || s.status === "Draft" || s.status === "Confirmed");
    if (filterCategory === "Attention") return subscriptions.filter((s) => s.status === "Past Due");
    if (filterCategory === "Paused") return subscriptions.filter((s) => s.status === "Paused");
    return subscriptions;
  }, [subscriptions, filterCategory]);

  // Lifecycle Transitions
  function transitionSubscription(id: string, nextStatus: LifecycleStatus, reason?: string) {
    const subscription = subscriptions.find((item) => item.id === id);
    if (!subscription) return;
    const allowedTransitions: Record<LifecycleStatus, LifecycleStatus[]> = {
      Draft: ["Quotation", "Closed"],
      Quotation: ["Confirmed", "Closed"],
      Confirmed: ["Active", "Closed"],
      Active: ["Paused", "Past Due", "Closed"],
      Paused: ["Active", "Closed"],
      "Past Due": ["Active", "Closed"],
      Closed: [],
    };
    if (!allowedTransitions[subscription.status].includes(nextStatus)) {
      showToast(`Cannot move ${id} from ${subscription.status} to ${nextStatus}.`);
      return;
    }
    setSubscriptions((prev) =>
      prev.map((sub) => {
        if (sub.id !== id) return sub;
        return {
          ...sub,
          status: nextStatus,
          dunningAttempts: nextStatus === "Active" ? 0 : sub.dunningAttempts,
        };
      })
    );
    showToast(`Subscription ${id} transitioned to ${nextStatus}${reason ? `: ${reason}` : ""}`);
  }

  // Invoice Actions
  function confirmInvoice(invoiceId: string) {
    setInvoices((prev) =>
      prev.map((inv) => (inv.id === invoiceId ? { ...inv, status: "Confirmed" } : inv))
    );
    showToast(`Invoice ${invoiceId} confirmed and queued for dispatch.`);
  }

  function sendInvoice(invoice: InvoiceItem) {
    const timeStr = new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" });
    setInvoices((prev) =>
      prev.map((inv) => (inv.id === invoice.id ? { ...inv, sentAt: `Today, ${timeStr}` } : inv))
    );
    showToast(`Invoice ${invoice.id} sent to ${invoice.customerEmail} via SMTP.`);
  }

  function downloadInvoiceCSV(invoiceId: string) {
    const invoice = invoices.find(({ id }) => id === invoiceId);
    if (!invoice) return;
    const content = [
      "Payloop Tax Invoice",
      `Invoice number,${invoice.id}`,
      `Customer,${invoice.customerName}`,
      `Description,${invoice.description}`,
      `Subtotal,₹${invoice.subtotal}`,
      `Tax (18%),₹${invoice.tax}`,
      `Total,₹${invoice.total}`,
      `Status,${invoice.status}`,
    ].join("\n");
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([content], { type: "text/csv" }));
    const anchor = document.createElement("a");
    anchor.href = url;
    anchor.download = `${invoice.id}.csv`;
    anchor.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    showToast(`${invoice.id} downloaded`);
  }

  function openPrintModal(invoice: InvoiceItem) {
    setActiveInvoiceForPrint(invoice);
    setPrintInvoiceModalOpen(true);
  }

  function openPaymentModalForInvoice(invoice: InvoiceItem) {
    setSelectedInvoiceForPayment(invoice);
    setPayAmount(invoice.total);
    setPayRef(`TXN-${Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000)}`);
    setPayNotes(`Settled by staff for ${invoice.id}`);
    setRecordPaymentModalOpen(true);
  }

  function handleRecordPaymentSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!selectedInvoiceForPayment) return;

    const amount = Number(payAmount);
    if (!Number.isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) {
      showToast("Enter a valid payment amount.");
      return;
    }
    if (amount !== selectedInvoiceForPayment.total) {
      showToast(`Record the full outstanding balance of ₹${selectedInvoiceForPayment.total.toLocaleString("en-IN")}.`);
      return;
    }
    if (!payRef.trim()) {
      showToast("Enter a transaction reference before recording payment.");
      return;
    }

    const newPayment: PaymentRecord = {
      id: `PAY-${Math.floor(9000 + Math.random() * 1000)}`,
      invoiceId: selectedInvoiceForPayment.id,
      customerName: selectedInvoiceForPayment.customerName,
      method: payMethod,
      amount,
      date: "31 Oct 2026",
      transactionRef: payRef,
      notes: payNotes,
    };

    setPayments((prev) => [newPayment, ...prev]);

    setInvoices((prev) =>
      prev.map((inv) =>
        inv.id === selectedInvoiceForPayment.id ? { ...inv, status: "Paid", daysOverdue: undefined } : inv
      )
    );

    // Auto-reactivate subscription if past due
    setSubscriptions((prev) =>
      prev.map((sub) => {
        if (sub.id === selectedInvoiceForPayment.subscriptionId && sub.status === "Past Due") {
          return { ...sub, status: "Active", dunningAttempts: 0 };
        }
        return sub;
      })
    );

    setRecordPaymentModalOpen(false);
    showToast(`Payment of ₹${Number(payAmount).toLocaleString("en-IN")} recorded! Invoice marked as Paid.`);
  }

  // Batch Billing Day Run (Simulation)
  function triggerBillingDay() {
    const billable = activeSubs.filter((sub) => !invoices.some(
      (invoice) => invoice.subscriptionId === sub.id && (invoice.status === "Draft" || invoice.status === "Confirmed")
    ));
    if (billable.length === 0) {
      showToast("Billing run complete: no new invoices were needed. Duplicate drafts were skipped.");
      return;
    }
    const issuedDate = "31 Oct 2026";
    const newInvoices = billable.map((sub, index): InvoiceItem => {
      const taxableSubtotal = Math.round(sub.totalCents / (1 + sub.taxRate));
      const subtotal = taxableSubtotal + sub.discountAmount;
      const tax = sub.totalCents - taxableSubtotal;
      return {
        id: `INV-2026-${1060 + invoices.length + index}`,
        subscriptionId: sub.id,
        customerName: sub.customerName,
        customerEmail: customers.find((customer) => customer.id === sub.customerId)?.email ?? "",
        customerTaxId: customers.find((customer) => customer.id === sub.customerId)?.taxId ?? "",
        description: `${sub.planName} · Scheduled renewal`,
        subtotal,
        tax,
        discount: sub.discountAmount,
        total: sub.totalCents,
        status: "Draft",
        issuedDate,
        dueDate: sub.expiryDate,
        idempotencyKey: `demo-billing-${sub.id}-2026-10-31`,
      };
    });
    setInvoices((previous) => [...newInvoices, ...previous]);
    showToast(`Billing run complete: ${newInvoices.length} draft invoice${newInvoices.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} created; duplicates skipped.`);
  }

  function useTemplate(template: QuoteTemplate) {
    setFormPlan(template.planName.includes("Flow") ? "Flow Analytics" : template.planName.includes("Enterprise") ? "Enterprise Suite" : "Studio Workspace");
    setFormInterval(template.billingInterval);
    setFormVariant(template.variant.includes("Odoo") ? "Odoo Brand (+₹560)" : "Standard");
    setFormPrioritySupport(template.addons.some((addon) => addon.includes("Priority")));
    setFormExtraStorage(template.addons.some((addon) => addon.includes("Storage")));
    setFormDiscount(template.discountName.startsWith("BULK20") ? "BULK20" : template.discountName.startsWith("FESTIVE10") ? "FESTIVE10" : "None");
    setCreateSubModalOpen(true);
    showToast(`${template.title} applied to the quote builder.`);
  }

  // Create Subscription Calculation
  const calculatedSubtotal = useMemo(() => {
    let base = 3500;
    if (formPlan === "Flow Analytics") base = 1180;
    if (formPlan === "Cloud Storage") base = 2000;
    if (formPlan === "Enterprise Suite") base = 15000;
    let variant = formVariant.includes("Odoo") ? 560 : 0;
    let addons = (formPrioritySupport ? 490 : 0) + (formExtraStorage ? 800 : 0);
    return base + variant + addons;
  }, [formPlan, formVariant, formPrioritySupport, formExtraStorage]);

  const calculatedDiscount = useMemo(() => {
    if (formDiscount === "FESTIVE10") return Math.round(calculatedSubtotal * 0.1);
    if (formDiscount === "BULK20") return 2000;
    return 0;
  }, [formDiscount, calculatedSubtotal]);

  const calculatedTax = useMemo(() => {
    const taxable = Math.max(0, calculatedSubtotal - calculatedDiscount);
    return Math.round(taxable * 0.18);
  }, [calculatedSubtotal, calculatedDiscount]);

  const calculatedTotal = useMemo(() => {
    return Math.max(0, calculatedSubtotal - calculatedDiscount) + calculatedTax;
  }, [calculatedSubtotal, calculatedDiscount, calculatedTax]);

  function handleCreateSubscription(statusToCreate: LifecycleStatus) {
    const cust = customers.find((c) => c.id === formCustomer) || customers[0];
    const newSub: SubscriptionItem = {
      id: `SUB-2026-0${940 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 50)}`,
      customerId: cust.id,
      customerName: cust.company,
      customerContact: cust.name,
      planName: formPlan,
      billingInterval: formInterval,
      variantName: formVariant,
      variantPrice: formVariant.includes("Odoo") ? 560 : 0,
      addons: [
        ...(formPrioritySupport ? ["Priority Support (+₹490)"] : []),
        ...(formExtraStorage ? ["Extra Storage (+₹800)"] : []),
      ],
      basePrice: calculatedSubtotal,
      taxRate: 0.18,
      discountAmount: calculatedDiscount,
      totalCents: calculatedTotal,
      status: statusToCreate,
      startDate: "01 Nov 2026",
      expiryDate: formInterval === "Yearly" ? "01 Nov 2027" : "01 Dec 2026",
      paymentTerms: formPaymentTerms,
      iconLetter: cust.company[0],
      iconTheme: "purple",
    };

    setSubscriptions((prev) => [newSub, ...prev]);
    setCreateSubModalOpen(false);
    showToast(`Created new ${statusToCreate.toLowerCase()} ${newSub.id} for ${cust.company}`);
  }

                      <strong>{sub.planName}</strong>

                      </span>
                    </div>
```
